PREPARED LOW-RANK NECROMANCY SOURCE CONFERENCE — NOT SENT.

# Low-rank Necromancy spell source audit

prepared-not-sent; source questions only

For every selected spell, settle all operational fields: actual activation and components, legal target/range/geometry, duration and concentration, resolution/DC/save or attack, full effect clauses, scaling, repeat casting/source identity, suppression/ending, limits and class-specific rank/cost access. Preserve printed clauses and every original source. Explicitly label NEW completions; no external-game import or automatic historical alias merge.

All30 selected identities retain their complete catalog record,30 exact Core rows and30 current-book placements. Soul Tap retains its HP-and-Resonance versus HP/mana wording conflict. Twenty-one operational comparison spells,seven pending spells and actual armor records accompany the questions. No spell is promoted by preparation.

| Case | Spell | Full-source question |
|---|---|---|
| NE-01 | Rotting Touch (rotting-touch) | Preserve Touch and exactly1 necrotic per round for3 rounds. Decide hostile application attack/save, first tick and all three native regular markers, repeat casting, suppression, concentration and removal. Separate a source damage sequence from disease, Bleeding, healing prohibition or physical decomposition; none follows merely from the name. No extra-turn or initiative-reset ticks. |
| NE-02 | Finger of the Dead (finger-of-the-dead) | Preserve1d4 necrotic and heal1HP. Define attack/save, healing recipient and whether the heal requires a hit, positive resolved damage or actual donor-funded HP loss. Resolve zeroHP, temp/formHP, floors, immunity, trivial/friendly targets and repeat casts using existing drain rules. Any finite donor/reserve or no-recursion integration must be explicit, preserving the promised heal rather than silently deleting it. |
| NE-03 | Corpse Light (corpse-light) | Preserve a real corpse glowing green. Specify eligible body/remains, light radius/intensity, lifetime despite Instantaneous, simultaneous casts, suppression and whether movement carries the light. Define effects of raising, resurrection, destruction or consumption of that same body. No automatic undead detection, corpse preservation, creature animation, information or origin/afterlife claim. |
| NE-04 | Whispers from Beyond (whispers-from-beyond) | Preserve WIS save and disadvantage on the next attack. Define eligible targets/senses, expiry of the unused mark, precise attack commitment and cancellation/automatic-result handling. Do not replace the attack penalty with Frightened or a forced action. Multiple same-source marks need finite arbitration without generating a second attack or importing a spirit-contact explanation. |
| NE-05 | Spectral Claw (spectral-claw) | Preserve1d6 slashing plus1 necrotic at the printed30ft range. Specify attack/save and a single mixed-type damage event with actual defense/critical rules for each component. Determine whether a visible claw exists only for resolution. No persistent weapon, extra natural attack, remote manipulator, physical claw material or automatic scaling solely from the name. |
| NE-06 | Gravewind Flicker (gravewind-flicker) | Preserve both pushing an object and shutting a door. Define finite target mass/size, displacement, range/line of effect, held/resisted objects and ordinary hinges, locks or obstructions. Keep actual custody and final supported placement. Decide occupied doorways and hazards without granting automatic crushing damage, unlocking, creature movement or persistent control. |
| NE-07 | Toll the Dead (toll-the-dead) | Preserve1d8 necrotic or1d12 if already injured. Define attack/save, the pre-event injured predicate and its snapshot across normalHP, formHP, tempHP and maximum changes. Wounds and current missingHP are different records; choose explicitly. No extra injury, implied cantrip scaling or stronger die based on damage inflicted by this same unresolved event. |
| NE-08 | Death Whisper (death-whisper) | Preserve one question to an actual corpse. Define body condition, age, language, possible answers/knowledge, response length, refusal/deception and a finite recast/corpse-family limit. Compare reviewed Speak with Dead and pending Lesser without merging identities. Describe only the playable information interface; do not settle soul location, afterlife, identity ownership or cosmic origin. |
| NE-09 | Bone Shard (bone-shard) | Preserve1d6 piercing and ignoring non-metal armor. Set exact attack resolution and substitute AC calculation, distinguishing armor base from DEX, shields, cover, natural armor, wards and mixed materials. Bind treatment to actual equipment records; names alone do not certify material. Define projectile/remains custody without granting free valuable bone/ammunition or ignoring every nonmetal defensive effect. |
| NE-10 | Wither Plant (wither-plant) | Preserve a nonmagical plant withering and dying. Define finite size/extent and whether rooted colonies, ordinary trees, cultivated crops or creature-stat plants qualify; establish resistance for any hostile creature target if allowed. Preserve actual remaining mass and ecological/harvest state. No automatic creature death by botanical name, poisoning, plant-material multiplication or new ingredient availability. |
| NE-11 | Blood Trickle (blood-trickle) | Preserve1 damage per round for3 rounds. Decide whether this is source-qualified Bleeding, damage type, eligible anatomy, application resolution, first tick and exactly three regular opportunities. Reconcile Medicine/healing cure endpoints, suppression and same-source replacement. Do not accidentally add generic Bleeding damage on top or turn repeat casts into extra native ticks. |
| NE-12 | Decay (decay) | Preserve immediate2d6 necrotic followed by1d6 each round for3 rounds. Define application attack/save, success branch, tick markers, concentration, repeated casts and ending. Distinguish this ordinary spell identity from the existing custom Decay healing prohibition; the shared name does not import that prohibition or erase the three later damage installments. |
| NE-13 | Dread Whisper (dread-whisper) | Preserve WIS save, flee for1round and1d4 psychic. Specify damage on each save outcome, finite forced movement direction/path/timing, available movement/actions, impassable or dangerous routes and opportunities. Reconcile current hard-denial and Frightened rules explicitly without silently granting Frightened or replacing the printed flight with only disadvantage. |
| NE-14 | Putrefy (putrefy) | Preserve the corpse becoming unusable for divine magic. Resolve a finite corpse-local mechanical blocker, duration, target eligibility, removal, destruction/reconstruction and interaction with current resurrection/animation/information effects. The term divine is not an existing universal source classification: use explicit reviewed source bindings or identify a precise deferred dependency. Do not define gods, souls, afterlife or cosmology, nor silently broaden this to every spell or identity-wide permanent denial. |
| NE-15 | Pact of Bone (pact-of-bone) | Preserve both+1AC and+2 necrotic resist. Reconcile the numeric resistance with current binary halving resistance through an explicit NEW rule, including damage ordering/rounding and modifier families. Define target, lifetime/concentration and recasting despite Instantaneous. No automatic bone armor, body change, healing, additional HP or extrapolated patron/pact cosmology. |
| NE-16 | Soul Tap (soul-tap) | Preserve the wording conflict: catalog says steal1d6HP and Resonance on hit; Core says steal1d6HP/mana on hit. Decide one roll versus separate quantities, HP/RP branches, damage type, hit resolution, actual donor funding and recipient caps with cost2RP paid first. Address temp/formHP, zero pools, immunity/floors, self/friendly targets and causal recursion. Do not invent a soul currency or treat the ambiguous mana wording as approved unlimited recovery. |
| NE-17 | Eyes of the Grave (eyes-of-the-grave) | Preserve undead detection within60ft. Specify instantaneous snapshot versus sustained duration, perception/line-of-effect/obstruction rules and exactly what information is learned: presence, direction, count or location. Use actual creature eligibility, not appearance or names. Distinguish reviewed Sense Undead; no automatic identity, alignment, motive, hidden lore or cosmic-origin revelation. |
| NE-18 | Ray of Sickness (ray-of-sickness) | Preserve2d8 poison damage and Poisoned on failed CON. Reconcile whether the save controls damage as well as the condition and whether any separate attack is required. Set finite Poisoned duration/end saves, immunity distinctions and repeated casts. No invented disease, poison dose material, extra damage pulses or automatic condition when the applicable save succeeds. |
| NE-19 | Inflict Wounds (inflict-wounds) | Preserve a Touch attack for3d10 necrotic with ordinary spell-attack and critical handling. Specify eligible targets and actual touch geometry. The Core Healing tag contains no healing clause: record that mismatch without inventing HP gain, corpse revival or wound cure. The name also does not grant an automatic Wound beyond the reviewed damage/Wound procedure. |
| NE-20 | Corpse Puppet (corpse-puppet) | Preserve animating a small corpse for simple tasks. Define exact body eligibility, task menu, profile, speed/load, commands, range, finite duration/concentration and number controlled. Integrate one real body identity and existing companion budgets where applicable, including damage/end/remains. Do not silently upgrade it to a combat minion, spellcaster, free skilled worker, living resurrection or renewable corpse/harvest donor. |
| NE-21 | Bone Armor (bone-armor) | Preserve armor from actual bones,+2AC for1hour. Specify bone quantity/mass, custody/consumption or return, legal wearer/fit, donning, armor-versus-ward classification and AC family with existing armor/shields. Resolve suppression, breakage, removal and expiry using the same material identity. No new bones, permanent marketable armor, healing, unused craft stock or durability refresh from recasting. |
| NE-22 | Speak with Dead (Lesser) (speak-with-dead-lesser) | Preserve exactly one question to a recently deceased corpse. Define recently with a finite elapsed window, body/knowledge/language/response rules and recast limits, comparing Death Whisper and reviewed Speak with Dead without automatic identity merge. Explain their source-specific mechanical differences. This does not decide soul availability, afterlife travel, divine authority or resurrection. |
| NE-23 | Summon Bone Archer (summon-bone-archer) | Preserve one undead archer,HP20,2d6 piercing and concentration up to1minute. Supply complete AC,speed,size,senses,saves,attack modifier/range,initiative and command/offense costs under current creature rules. Define creation space, replacement/count, ammunition, ending/remains and resource provenance. Keep summon origin unresolved cosmologically; no extra command pool, renewable corpse fuel or free salvage/ammunition. |
| NE-24 | Ghoul Touch (ghoul-touch) | Preserve1d8 necrotic and Paralyzed on failed CON at Touch. Clarify any attack requirement, damage on save outcomes, finite paralysis duration, repeat saves/removal and current hard-denial scope. Resolve separate condition/damage immunity. No unprinted poison disease, stench aura, indefinite paralysis or delayed extra attacks inherited from an external version. |
| NE-25 | Cursed Soulrot (cursed-soulrot) | Preserve1d6 necrotic per round for5 rounds. Define application attack/save, exactly five native markers, concentration, repeated casts and suppression/removal. Decide explicit Curse typing if any; name alone cannot select curse-removal eligibility or create a soul injury. No permanent maxHP loss, healing denial or skipped-turn catch-up damage without a new source decision. |
| NE-26 | Drain Vigor (drain-vigor) | Preserve-2STR and2d6 necrotic as separate clauses. Define attack/save and outcomes, penalty duration/concentration, score floor and derived checks/attacks/damage/carrying, same-source stacking and restoration. Distinguish capacity changes from damage or healing. The word drain grants no caster HP/RP/stat increase, permanent advancement loss or automatic incapacitation. |
| NE-27 | Gentle Repose (gentle-repose) | Preserve a corpse from decay through a finite target/duration/concentration decision despite Instantaneous. State exactly which physical-age/harvest clocks pause and how elapsed time resumes. Distinguish corpse condition from elapsed resurrection/question windows; extending those requires explicit authority. Retain body identity and prior damage/age, without restoring consumed remains, producing repeat harvests or deciding afterlife/soul state. |
| NE-28 | Ray of Enfeeblement (ray-of-enfeeblement) | Preserve halving damage from the target's Strength attacks. Define attack/save, lifetime/concentration, repeat endings and the actual Strength-attack predicate, including mixed damage/riders. Specify rounding and ordering with resistance/other reductions. No STR score reduction, halving every spell or environmental packet, HP loss, condition or new damage because Core tags this Damage. |
| NE-29 | Blindness/Deafness (blindness-deafness) | Preserve choice of Blinded or Deafened and CON negates. Set choice timing, finite lifetime/concentration, repeat saves/removal and same-source replacement when modes differ. Apply actual reviewed condition/sense rules and source-specific immunity. No both-modes default, eye/ear destruction, perception through walls or loss of unrelated alternate senses. |
| NE-30 | Wither (wither) | Preserve a limb becoming withered and weak. Give a finite eligible-anatomy/limb selection, attack/save, duration/concentration and exact bounded mechanical penalty with end/removal. Compare Disable Limb and Drain Vigor rather than merging them. Do not infer permanent amputation, automatic Wound, paralysis, item loss or repeated penalties for the same anatomy from the sparse wording. |

The four prepared cohorts now cover all108 currently unfinished identities with a minimum class-access rank of0,1 or2, preserving111 current-book placements. This is preparation coverage, not spell completion:276 of848 ordinary identities remain operational and572 unfinished. Existing guides and original sources are unchanged.

The current Fishing source conference remains active. These questions are unsent. Corpse and recovery interfaces can be discussed mechanically; gods, souls, afterlife and cosmic origin remain for the careful review after the authorized GitHub push and remote verification.

guide","exactText":"# Spell integration rules — provisional v0.1\n\nUse this document with the [shared class rules](CLASS_PLAYTEST_RULES.md), the class's own engine and the [spell packet](SPELL_PLAYTEST_STARTER.md). This is a staged development layer, not an approved-book replacement or a live companion update.\n\n## What owns each decision\n\n| Decision | Source used for this packet |\n|---|---|\n| Spell identity, every school placement and per-class rank/access | Preserved `src/data/spells.js`; do not collapse different class ranks into one global rank. |\n| Common RP prices and additional spell evidence | Core Systems Known Descent V2. Its spell blocks are retained with the catalog summaries. |\n| Class identity, engine, spellbook/preparation and cadence exceptions | The current class playtest packet. |\n| Generic Dissonance/Refrain, action limits and core conditions | Shared class playtest rules; source Core conditions remain unchanged. |\n| Missing targeting, exact endpoints, concentration ambiguities and exploit limits | Explicitly provisional decisions on each spell card. |\n\nThe approved Core V2 source has 936 spell blocks. The companion catalog has 848 unique IDs with 936 school placements, but leaves four metadata fields unspecified. These are different views of the source, not evidence that all casting details were absent. Core's generic “Automatic or opposed as described” does not supply a concrete save or targeting rule.\n\nEleven possible renamed entries remain comparative evidence in the register. Harmonic Bulwark's catalog summary says 5 temporary HP per RP while the possible historical Mana Shield entry says 2; its [individual transformation and buffer review](TRANSFORMATION_SPELL_PLAYTEST.md) now supplies the operational decision. Both source variants remain preserved. Other unresolved aliases stay pending their own review; matching names never silently merge effects.\n\nResonance Kindle now has [exact manuscript approval](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_CONJURATION_CANTRIP_EXACT_REVIEW.md): Action/Self/V/S, recover 1 RP only when this spell reaches resolution. Its one character-wide use is spent at legal cast start and refreshes only on a beneficial completed long rest. The comparative Mana Kindle block remains separately preserved; its broader wording does not trigger recovery from other cantrips and its historical range grants no remote recipient. The [Conjuration cantrip status](CONJURATION_CANTRIP_REVIEW_STATUS.json) records this bounded decision and the other eleven approved cards.\n\n## Select a spell\n\n1. Choose the class through which you are learning/casting it. Check that class's school access and minimum **class** level on the card.\n2. Use that class's spell rank and Core V2 cost. A different eligible class may receive the same spell at a different rank and level.\n3. Record it in your allowed known, prepared or spellbook choices. An eligible catalog entry is not an automatic grant. Wizard and Druid keep their explicit preparation systems; fallback counts apply only where the class has no more specific rule.\n4. Select only a spell marked `operational-playtest-draft` in this packet, or an operational spell expressly supplied by a class card. Other records stay visible in the unfinished queue.\n5. A class card and an ordinary spell with the same name remain separate records with separate acquisition and costs. Knowing one does not grant the other. Oath spells, Matrix techniques and similar class spells use their own card.\n\nOracle uses RC and its Mystery/Curse system. Soldier and Warrior have no general Weave access. Dragoon first gains ordinary magic at class 3; Paladin at class 2. Sufficient RP, total character level, a class feature with the same name, or a borrowed spellbook cannot bypass a gate.\n\n## Resolve a cast at the table\n\n1. Verify the available Action/Bonus Action/Reaction, its exact trigger, components, targets, range and line of effect. No legal target means no activation or payment. A target in an area still needs the card's eligibility. Total cover blocks direct targeting unless expressly overridden.\n2. Choose a spell from your actual repertoire. Determine its class-specific rank and RP cost: rank 0–9 costs **0/2/3/5/7/10/13/17/21/26**. Add explicitly printed extra costs; Resonance Shield adds 1 RP even when the caster receives it as a cantrip. Generic Overcast is excluded.\n3. Read Dissonance **before** casting. Limit = max(4, 2 + PB + max(0, casting modifier)). At limit/limit+3/limit+6, ranked spells cost +1/+2/+3 RP. At limit+6 ordinary rank-1+ casting is blocked. Cantrips have no strain surcharge but still build Refrain. An expressly granted traumatic-casting exception pays the cost and its printed trauma; it grants neither rank nor repertoire access.\n4. Commit the legal cast's RP, materials and other printed costs before any attack/save or interruption. Perform its components and establish concentration if required, ending incompatible prior concentration. An ordinary missed, saved-against or countered legal cast refunds nothing and never restores displaced concentration.\n5. At this cast's beginning, apply ordinary Dissonance and Refrain once: 0 base for rank 0, 1 for ranks 1–5, 2 for ranks 6–9; consecutive same-named casts add Refrain +1/+2/+3, capped at +3. First cast or a different name adds +0. Apply the class's exact protected reduction, routing or vent and any hook whose printed trigger is beginning/casting. Weapon or idle turns do not reset the previous name/Refrain unless the class expressly says so. This chronological commitment occurs before the Counterspell window; a later Reaction cast updates its own history, and resuming the earlier cast does not update that history again.\n6. Open legal cast-interruption Reactions, resolving nested counters last-in, first-out. A surviving cast then resolves its effect: spell attacks are d20 + PB + the actual casting ability modifier; save DC is 8 + PB + that modifier. Use the exact card's hit/failure/success results and ordinary damage/conditions. Outcome-dependent class hooks occur only at their printed timing and with the required actual result. No automatic cantrip scaling applies. A negated spell grants no success/hit/damage effect merely because its casting cost was paid.\n7. Record concentration, exact expiration, per-rest use, summon and companion command obligations. A second spell cannot supply an ungranted Action, duplicate concentration or resource reset. A readied spell commits this casting sequence at preparation; release has no second cast, cost, cadence update or Counterspell window.\n\nCountering requires an actual operational spell or class card, its repertoire/access and a legal remaining Reaction. The ordinary Counterspell is now reviewed; it is separate from the Mage and Wizard class cards. Dispel Minor, Dispel Resonance, Dispel Magic (Lesser) and Dispel Magic use their exact individual scopes. Rank-limited dispels inspect the original generating cast's spell rank, not RP cost or the dispeller's rank. Higher and unassigned source variants retain their own unfinished status.\n\n## Counterspell checks and interruption\n\nThe [exact notebook review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_ABJURATION_RANK2_3_EXACT_REVIEW.md) confirms this authored timing interface and corrects the earlier binary-check proposal using the existing [Core roll rules](CORE_RESOLUTION_PLAYTEST.md). This is a tabletop procedure; the pricing helper does not implement nested reactions.\n\nOrdinary Counterspell uses its casting class's actual spellcasting ability + PB against **DC 10 + twice the triggering spell's actual rank**. The class Wizard/Mage cards retain their printed Intelligence + PB check and their own acquisition, cost, rank and recharge. Apply the existing fixed-DC task degrees and natural shifts, then collapse Critical Success/Success to success and Failure/Critical Failure to failure. No extra critical counter effect is invented. It is neither a spell attack nor a saving throw.\n\n| Natural d20 + modifier | DC | Existing task result | Counter result |\n|---|---:|---|---|\n| 20 + 0 | 22 | Failure improves to Success | Success |\n| 1 + 9 | 10 | Success worsens to Failure | Failure |\n| 1 + 20 | 10 | Critical Success worsens to Success | Success |\n\nThe target must be an actual pending spell with a recorded rank. The Director may use hidden source data without revealing the spell's identity/rank; attempting grants no free identification. An unassigned rank is not automatically zero. A source-defined technique is not counterable merely because it spends RP or resembles magic.\n\nA creature normally attempts Counterspell once against a particular pending cast, across its ordinary and same-named class sources. Another Reaction alone does not supply another attempt. An exact source expressly granting an additional attempt against that same cast overrides the default. After failure, another eligible creature may react; after negation there is no further trigger. Counterspell is itself a spell and can be countered. Resolve nested counters last-in, first-out and resume surviving parents without recasting, paying again, or replaying their earlier Refrain/Dissonance. Negation refunds no costs and restores no prior concentration. Countering one spell is not whole-Action/turn hard denial and does not spend that combined ledger.\n\n\n## Reviewed placement-specific cards\n\nDetect Poison has Divination rank-0 and Abjuration rank-1 modes. The casting class's preserved school/rank selects the mode; the rank-1 mode also requires F. Alignment Shield has Abjuration rank-1/30-foot and Conjuration rank-3/60-foot modes; its 60-foot header represents only the maximum range across modes. Read each full target and resolution field. These are manuscript rules; the pricing helper does not execute targeting or components.\n\nRepulsion Field's operational entry is only the rank-1 object pulse. Its distinct rank-6 creature effect remains preserved but unavailable. Warding Bond uses its preserved rank-1 class access; the rank-2 duplicate grants no extra effect. Higher character level or extra RP never grants a blocked placement or changes modes. All source variants remain in the catalog's source evidence.\n\nAlignment Sanctuary has Abjuration rank-3 admission saves and the actually granted Shaman Conjuration rank-6 matching-alignment mode. The unassigned rank-6 Abjuration benefits remain unavailable. Identity counts do not close every preserved placement; consult the [variant register](SPELL_VARIANT_REVIEW_STATUS.json).\n\nOrdinary Warding Bond follows the reviewed damage-sharing stage/root rules and its exact card. It does not use or enlarge the Paladin class redirection round ledger or grant named Paladin healing/resource riders. Its authored promotion does not establish an implementation of its effect in the damage-sharing reference helper.\n\n## Reviewed finite conjurations\n\nThe [rank-1 Conjuration review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_CONJURATION_RANK1_EXACT_REVIEW.md) selects **2 RP** for Summon Mod Rod under the manifest's Core V2 spellcasting precedence; the conflicting V6/catalog 1-RP wording remains preserved. Its charge and separate character-wide caster supply/recipient receipt uses commit at activation start, including prevented or zero recovery, and refresh only on each character's beneficial completed long rest. Replacing rods never resets those records.\n\nRead the [exact cards](SPELL_PLAYTEST_STARTER.md) for each created object's weight, native HP, duration, custody, load and ending. Summon Minor Weapon is wielded through ordinary weapon actions; it grants no attack on casting. Conjure Rope and Resonance Bridge require real support and apply ordinary Falling on failure. Alignment Essence preserves its empty result-vial for one minute after full consumption. Faction Messenger conserves supplied mundane paper and cannot resolve an ambiguous recipient description magically. Heat Metal keeps its physical-item round record and six-second next-pulse deadline through recasting, handoff and initiative changes. These are authored procedures; [review status](CONJURATION_RANK1_REVIEW_STATUS.json) does not certify runtime or balance.\n\n## Components, summons and ongoing effects\n\n**V** requires audible speech; **S** requires a free hand or a class feature expressly permitting the gesture; **F** is the class's ordinary casting focus. Core's M/F shorthand is interpreted here as the existing focus alternative. No unspecified costly or consumed material is added. These hand/focus conventions are provisional and may require equipment integration. A spell with V is blocked by an effect explicitly preventing verbal casting.\n\nSummon stat blocks use their printed AC/HP/attack die. Where absent, saves use the owner's PB, other ability checks use +0, walking Speed is 30 feet, and there are no unstated creature immunities or extraordinary senses. Unless a card explicitly gives another bounded range, a commanded companion must be within 60 feet and able to perceive its owner. All one owner's companions share one Bonus Action command and **one damaging action total per round**. Without a command they move and Dodge. A replacement requires the full casting cost. No summon grants loot, a reusable corpse or resource-generating death trigger.\n\nThe [rank-2 Conjuration review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_CONJURATION_RANK2_EXACT_REVIEW.md) approves this specific companion overlay: **when an exact companion source prints multiple attacks as one damaging action, those attacks are components of one commanded offensive action and consume the owner's single offensive-companion allowance unless that source expressly grants otherwise.** Summon Air Pet explicitly makes its two source attacks within that one action; no second command/action or remaining offense for another companion is created. Its fixed AC15/HP20/two 1d6 attacks do not inherit generic companion scaling.\n\nPlanar Ally (Minor) uses a finite NEW summon-only profile for the source's intended CR-1 role. The bounded public bestiary scan did not establish an eligible published exact-CR1 extraplanar creature; this card does not certify its CR or rewrite a bestiary entry. Summon Weaponry Satchel has finite conjured contents tied to one duration, with a narrow exception only for an already resolving immediate use. Alignment Weapon's suppression default applies only to that printed card.\n\nResonance Well rolls one 1d4 total reserve. Its separately named creation and receipt uses commit before prevention and reset only through each character's beneficial long rest. These limits neither share nor reset Kindle or Mod Rod records. Read the exact cards and [rank-2 status](CONJURATION_RANK2_REVIEW_STATUS.json); pricing/schema checks do not execute these effects or certify balance.\n\nThe [rank-3 Conjuration review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_CONJURATION_RANK3_EXACT_REVIEW.md) adds seven bounded cards. Earth Pet's knockdown is Prone, not positional movement; generic push/pull immunity does not automatically prevent it. Burst of Flamewind affects every creature the caster ordinarily classifies as an enemy at a known location in its cone, with automatic source damage and a save controlling only its push.\n\nElemental Displacement selects a nearby willing recipient and a perceived destination; its teleport path is not constrained by intervening terrain. Spectral Steed actually crosses planes to a known site, once per steed attempt. Its 350-pound **and** 12-Load limits count the rider's body and all gear, including melded equipment. The reviewed correction from the conference's proposed6Load avoids excluding all gear from a Medium rider; ordinary Burdened applies at6Load. Read the exact mount/command/arrival procedure; it does not close general mounted combat.\n\nResonance Conduit transfers already-existing RP between two separate pools, with committed deductions, gated credit, and no resource creation or daily-ledger reset. Renewed consent cannot revive an ended application, but a new legal paid cast may create a new one. Faction Portal provides actual one-way travel to a known HQ on the same or another plane. Its positive-standing predicate uses the current canonical reputation record without migrating its scale. Travel may bypass a geographic route; elapsed clocks, cargo custody, contamination, claims, arrival law and settlement remain actual procedures. These cards do not declare automatic Extraction success or invent a blanket prohibition on planar escape. [Review status](CONJURATION_RANK3_REVIEW_STATUS.json) preserves the manuscript/runtime/balance distinction.\n\n Noncombat helpers cannot attack or operate combat items.\n\nA cantrip that stabilizes restores no HP and removes no Wound. Mending cannot replace finite machine repair or refresh ammunition, charges, duration or class resources. Created tools, servants and water obey the explicit card limits; a spell does not silently pay wages, produce saleable materials or erase abstract expedition Supply.\n\nAll hard whole-Action/turn denial uses one combined **source-character/target/encounter** record across that character's spells, class features and companions. Once used, later hard-denial riders from that source fail; damage and soft riders resolve as printed. Boss immunities remain effective. Same-stat flat bonuses use the strongest; same-name dice bonuses do not stack. Apply one allowed bonus-die spell per roll in this packet unless a reviewed feature explicitly permits more. Temporary HP never add together. Copies never generate copies, class procs or resource loops.\n\n## Falling bridge — new provisional procedure\n\nThis is a newly authored table procedure agreed with the GPT notebook, **not recovered V6/Core V2 canon**. Focused searches of the current rulebook extract, Core V2 and mechanics/manual text found spell summaries but no operational falling clock or impact formula. Before promotion to canon, inspect the approved V6 environment/travel/hazard sections and archived environment tables. Preserve any older rule found and compare it explicitly.\n\nWhen a creature loses support with no movement mode or effect keeping it aloft, it enters **Falling** immediately. Falling means unsupported; it grants no hovering, airborne walking or lateral movement. Track total distance descended during this fall and cumulative distance during its current uncontrolled portion, both initially zero.\n\n**Timing:** If a fall begins during a regular initiative turn, set a fixed Fall Marker at the **end of that initiative slot in the following round**. Resolve the first Falling Step there, then one at the same marker each round. Reactions and extra turns use the enclosing regular initiative slot; an event between slots uses the most recently completed slot. Extra turns, extra Actions and initiative changes neither move nor multiply the marker. This gives every participant one normal turn opportunity before the first descent; inability to act still matters and no free rescue action is granted. A caster still needs legal targets within range when spending their Action.\n\nOutside initiative, when timing matters, establish an order before descent and use a virtual slot immediately before its first regular slot as the initiating slot. The first step occurs when that virtual slot next recurs, after everyone has had one normal turn opportunity. When no rescue or other timing decision is possible, the Director can resolve the same descent and impact calculation directly. This is an explicit timing abstraction, not a model of physical acceleration.\n\nIf a fall begins after initiative has been established but before any regular initiative slot has yet completed, treat the virtual pre-round slot as the most recently completed slot for Fall Marker purposes.\n\n**Ordinary descent:** At each step descend up to **300 feet**, stopping on a supporting surface. Add the distance to both trackers. On landing while uncontrolled, take **min(10, floor(uncontrolled distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning damage**. The uncontrolled distance includes earlier steps; never use only the final step's remainder.\n\n**Feather Fall:** The spell retains its **Action** casting time and reduces descent to at most **60 feet per step**. When it takes effect, set uncontrolled distance to zero: the magic dissipates prior uncontrolled momentum in this abstraction. Keep total fall distance. If landing while it remains active, damage is **min(3, floor(total fall distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning**. This explicitly limits impact exposure without guaranteeing a harmless landing. When the spell ends aloft, reset uncontrolled distance to zero, then accumulate ordinary descent from that point at the existing marker; expiry grants no immediate extra step. Its one-minute maximum ends immediately before the caster's regular turn ten rounds after casting. If expiry and a step coincide, resolve expiry first. Earlier landing or cessation of Falling ends the spell on that target as printed.\n\nApply damage defenses only when their actual wording covers that damage. After damage, Falling ends; a creature suffering any positive falling damage lands **Prone** unless an explicit feature prevents it. Less than 20 relevant feet produces zero damage. Forced movement that removes support starts a fall but adds no separate fall damage. Regaining genuine support, secure support from a hold, or flight ends Falling without resolving an uncompleted impact; teleporting safely onto support likewise ends it. A later loss of support begins a new fall with new trackers and marker. Completed impact and damage cannot be undone by later casting.\n\n| Scenario | Steps to land | Impact |\n|---|---:|---:|\n| Ordinary 10 feet | 1 | 0 |\n| Ordinary 20 feet | 1 | 1d6 |\n| Ordinary 300 feet | 1 | 10d6 |\n| Ordinary 310 feet | 2 | 10d6 |\n| Ordinary 610 feet | 3 | 10d6 |\n| Feather Fall active through a 310-foot fall | 6 | 3d6 |\n| 120 feet slowed, spell ends aloft, then 190 feet uncontrolled | 3 | 9d6 |\n\nThe 300-foot ordinary step, impact formulas, momentum reset, marker and duration conventions are provisional inventions. The source supplies Feather Fall's Action, five targets and 60-foot slowed descent. Wider hazards, equipment catches, flight loss and environmental damage still need the combat/campaign pass.\n\n## Recovery bridge and unresolved integration\n\nThe class suite's **Retune** formula is the active RP rule for this combined test: recover max(0, floor(maxRP/4 + 0.5) + actual casting modifier), capped at maximum, and clear Dissonance after a completed one-hour short rest. Retain the expedition rule of at most **two beneficial short rests between long rests**, and one beneficial eight-hour long rest per **24 hours**. A long rest restores RP and clears Dissonance; expedition time, supply, pressure, Wounds and rest legality still apply. Count time once, not once in each tracker. Oracle and other non-RP engines use their class-specific recovery, not this formula.\n\nThis bridge explicitly chooses the class Retune formula over the gameplay draft's quarter-pool-only RP paragraph. Both originals remain preserved. A completed long rest also resets the remembered spell name and Refrain counter. A short rest clears Dissonance through Retune but does not reset Refrain unless a class feature expressly says so. This reset convention is an explicit provisional decision agreed in the notebook review. Broader recovery decisions for all class resources still require the character/campaign integration pass.\n\n## Developer verification\n\n`tools/spell_playtest_rules.py` checks operational status, class access, level gates, known/prepared membership and the **common pricing step**. Its Dissonance result is deliberately labelled *before class interface*. The caller must apply the actual protected class cadence or vent and verify actions, components, target legality, concentration and effect resolution. This helper is not an automatic implementation of every spell or class.\n\nRun `python tools/build_spell_playtests.py`, then `python tools/validate_spell_playtests.py`. Source accounting, metadata coverage, access boundaries and cost/reference scenarios are independently reported. A passing result does not certify the remaining catalog, all cross-class combinations, human balance or live-app readiness.\n\n`tools/fall_playtest_rules.py` separately exercises the new falling marker and distance/impact calculation. Callers still own actual spell availability/expiry, support, actions, damage rolls, defenses and Prone. Its regression cases specifically prevent premature or duplicate steps, harmless remainder exploits and retroactive changes to resolved landings.\n\n\n## Reviewed Divination cantrip information boundaries\n\nThe [eleven-card review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_DIVINATION_CANTRIP_EXACT_REVIEW.md) and [final source clarification](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_DIVINATION_CANTRIP_FINAL_CLARIFICATION.md) make each spell's information output explicit. Actual hidden facts may be revealed when a spell authorizes them; undefined records must not be invented. Sense Alignment distinguishes recorded axes, Indeterminate and genuinely unaligned objects. Detect Faction requires an actual affiliation/nonaffiliation record; absence of a field is not a negative finding. Resonance Sight reads current RP only, using ordinary visibility and actual pools; unreadability is not proof of no pool.\n\nProtection from Scrying prevents new information about its subject within its actual generating-school scope, including new Far Echo retrieval. It does not erase events, ordinary recordings or known facts, nor grant blanket immunity to Foresight Flicker's roll modifier or Glimpse Future's initiative benefit. Soul Mark's record can remain while its directional information is blocked. The final clarification retains the source's creature eligibility: active creature-classified Undead/Construct targets are not excluded merely by type. Death/destruction/ended summoned existence ends the mark; ordinary shapechange or travel alone does not.\n\nOmen Whisper limits materially equivalent inquiries across casters, while genuinely different material questions can receive another clue. Far Echo uses speech produced in its fixed capture sphere and actually audible at the center in the six seconds before casting. Read Surface Thoughts yields one actual current conscious thought after its save, not memories or forced truth. Detect Weather supplies an accurate current-trajectory forecast; a later actual new cause may alter it, with that cause recorded in the game. These are authored procedures, not a complete information/weather engine or balance certification. See [review status](DIVINATION_CANTRIP_REVIEW_STATUS.json).\n\n\n## Mark Omen and Fate Coin: reviewed spell-specific rerolls\n\nMark Omen and Fate Coin share one guard: an original roll event and its entire replacement lineage may receive at most one reroll from either of these two spells, across all casters, coins, marks and recasts. Reserve this allowance at the first legal activation's commitment, not after the replacement die resolves. For a multi-die roll, the whole set/pool is one original roll event; the resulting attack's separate later damage roll is a different event. A shared area-damage roll remains one event even when applied to several creatures. Other exact class reroll features keep their own permissions and timing, but do not erase this guard or create a fresh lineage for these two spells. If competing declarations are simultaneous, use current initiative order; outside initiative use a fixed neutral table order chosen before the roll. Only the first legal commitment spends a mark/coin; a later declaration barred by this guard spends nothing. No new Action, roll event, cast, payment or duplicated outcome hook is created by replacing dice.\n\nThe [rank-1 Divination exact review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_DIVINATION_RANK1_EXACT_REVIEW.md) requires Fate Coin to wait until the **complete original roll event** is rolled and its faces are available under normal table information rules. It then replaces only one chosen die before consequences apply. Mark Omen replaces the caster’s complete attack d20 procedure before hit/miss resolution. Neither creates another attack/cast, rerolls an established resource reserve, reverses applied consequences or duplicates costs/hooks. A Well’s initial reserve die may be changed before the reserve is established. These two spell grants do not rewrite the Oracle, Mirrorsage or other class reroll contracts.\n\nThe same review preserves actual distant hearing, concealed-entrance revelation, creature sight through non-solid obscurity, willing memory-sharing, and actual faction/alignment/Corruption information. Warning Ward uses established hostility and an entry-triggered six-elapsed-second glow; it does not infer hostility from creature type or faction. Alignment Sense retains its source-defined rank-1 redundancy as an open balance question. [Review status](DIVINATION_RANK1_REVIEW_STATUS.json) records manuscript scope; no full information/roll-system runtime or human balance is certified.\n\n\n## Rank-two Divination: paid information and preserved turn timing\n\nThe [exact review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_DIVINATION_RANK2_EXACT_REVIEW.md) requires **Locate Person, Faction Spy and Resonance Tracking** to resolve hidden person eligibility, plane/range conditions and trace existence after casting costs commit. Their legal Self inquiry/search must not reveal those facts through a free target-legality check. No-result feeds/bearings/searches do not diagnose the hidden reason or refund committed costs. This clarification is scoped to these exact card interfaces; other spells retain their printed target rules.\n\nThe [final timing clarification](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_DIVINATION_RANK2_FINAL_CLARIFICATION.md) keeps **Vision Trap** consistent with the existing hostile/next-turn convention: its pending rider ends at the end of the target's first future regular turn, excluding the entire current target turn even if its end is still upcoming. An attack earlier than that endpoint can consume the rider normally. Existing virtual markers, outside-initiative six-second timing and end-phase-before-cleanup ordering remain unchanged.\n\nAugury and Alignment Prediction supply bounded useful likelihood forecasts without forcing choices. Divine Perception requires actual divine provenance. Clairvoyance and Faction Spy provide only their specified fixed remote senses. Resonance Tracking preserves the original cast-start age of actual origin/path/endpoint traces; observing them creates no copied trail, new resource or refreshed age. [Review status](DIVINATION_RANK2_REVIEW_STATUS.json) records manuscript scope; wider information, provenance, runtime and numerical balance remain open.\n\n\n## Rank-three Divination: initiative, remains and information\n\nThe [exact review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_DIVINATION_RANK3_EXACT_REVIEW.md) approves all eight cards without functional repairs. Natural 1 always misses; natural 20 always hits and critically doubles damage dice, not flat modifiers. Beating AC by 10 is not a critical hit. An explicit broader critical range does not stack with another; use the broadest eligible range. In this overlay, a natural result below 20 in that range must still meet AC unless the feature expressly says it always hits.\n\n**Task, skill or tool check against a fixed DC:** total at least DC+10 is Critical Success; at least DC is Success; at most DC-10 is Critical Failure; other totals below DC are Failure. Natural 20 then improves one degree and natural 1 worsens one, bounded at either end. A task printing only success/failure collapses the two upper degrees to success and two lower degrees to failure; no extra consequence is invented. A natural 20 can still fail an otherwise impossible check, and a natural 1 with sufficient modifiers can still succeed.\n\n**Saving throw:** total at least DC succeeds; otherwise it fails. Do not split one packet to multiply Wound saves or combine separate hits merely to cross a threshold. Feature-specific prevention that acts before damage or before reaching zero resolves at its printed timing first.\n\n1. Resolve attack/save, damage and applicable defenses. The remaining nonnegative amount is **resolved damage**.\n2. Record HP, Wounds and zero-HP state before the event. Remove temporary HP first. **Post-temp damage = resolved damage minus temporary HP absorbed.** Keep this amount even when it exceeds current HP; floor the resulting current HP at zero.\n3. Check V6 Instant Death. If post-temp damage minus HP before the hit is at least maximum HP, the creature dies. For a creature already at zero, this is post-temp damage at least maximum HP. Stop: there is no Wound save, death-save failure or successful knockout from that event.\n4. Otherwise, a living creature makes one Wound save if it dropped from positive HP to zero, if **2 × post-temp damage is at least maximum HP**, or if the effect actually inflicts Maiming. Overlapping triggers still cause only one save. The post-temp threshold and one-save rule are new provisional interpretations. Maiming is an independent trigger even when temporary HP absorbs all ordinary damage; immunity to Maiming itself still applies.\n5. A Wound gained now cannot penalize the roll that created it.\n6. A success adds no Wound. A failure that began with zero, one or two Wounds adds exactly one; failure by 5 or more also gives that Wound one Injury. A failure that began with three Wounds kills the creature instead: no fourth Wound and no Injury roll. Stop processing later living-target consequences if dead.\n7. This is not one attack per entire turn or round.\n- **Range:** the first number is normal range, the second maximum. Beyond normal range gives disadvantage; beyond maximum is illegal. Ordinary close-range ranged-attack rules still apply.\n\nAfter an encounter, one uninterrupted minute searching accessible locations recovers `floor(eligible ordinary ammunition expended in that encounter / 2)`. Resolve recovery once for that encounter's expended ammunition; another search cannot recover another half. Only physically accessible, intact ordinary ammunition qualifies. These procedures connect the [Druid form rules](FORM_TEMP_HP_PLAYTEST.md) and [transformation spells](TRANSFORMATION_SPELL_PLAYTEST.md). They apply to the same character, HP record and physical belongings throughout a change. They grant no additional forms, spell access, actions or resources. The approved books remain preserved.\n\n## Entering and changing forms\n\nBefore paying for a voluntary change, choose a legal form and size, check its space at your current position, and identify any equipment that must be dropped. If the destination or required drops are illegal, the change does not begin and spends nothing. Otherwise pay the source's normal costs and resolve its actual entry effects once. A successful hostile or forced transformation cannot be prevented merely by excess gear: resolve it under its source rules, then reconcile excess equipment by keeping it external, dropping it or recording pending placement. Other source requirements still apply.\n\nYour Druid form uses your own HP, ability scores, saves, proficiency and conditions. It supplies only its printed anatomy and features. Speech does not imply hands: none of the 26 current Druid profiles grants ordinary hand-based equipment use. Of the 12 spell profiles, only Humanoid grants hands. Each profile retains its exact size and movement permissions.\n\n| Change | Equipment procedure |\n|---|---|\n| Root body to first Druid form | Record and meld the equipment physically worn, held or carried by your body, including carried containers and their contents. |\n| Druid form to another Druid form | Keep the same original melded equipment. End the former form's dependent effects and start the new form under its normal rules. Reconcile newly acquired external items with the new anatomy. |\n| A successful spell replaces a Druid or spell form | Close the former transformation and capture the legitimate resulting gear under the new spell's meld rules in one continuous change. There is no moment when you can use the returning equipment. |\n| Shapechange changes its current spell profile | Keep compatible external items. Drop incompatible external items; they do not join the original melded equipment. |\n| Suppression | Follow the spell's suppression procedure. It is not an ending or a fresh capture of equipment. Original melded gear stays inaccessible. |\n| Final reversion, expiry or forced ending | Return to root anatomy, end the source's dependent effects and reconcile surviving equipment as described below. |\n\nThe continuing meld record across Druid-to-Druid changes is a **new provisional integration rule**. It prevents shifting from opening an equipment-use window between forms. A spell replacement retains the already-reviewed close-and-capture procedure.\n\n## What melds\n\nRecord where each item was before the first change: worn in a particular slot, held in a particular hand, or carried in a particular container or on the body. Ownership alone is insufficient. A sword stored in a wagon stays in the wagon when its owner becomes a Hawk. A legitimately borrowed sword in your actual custody can meld and return normally; the lender remains its owner. Changing ownership metadata does not itself move an inaccessible physical item.\n\nA backpack and each of its contents remain distinct items. Count each item's own weight or Load once; a container does not erase its contents. Melded items remain in your custody but are inaccessible. You cannot withdraw or add contents, use components, consume supplies, fire ammunition or activate equipment through the meld.\n\nMelding never repairs damage, reloads a weapon, restores charges, replenishes supplies or creates a replacement item. Destruction, consumption and legitimate transfers remain real. Keep the current condition and quantity of each actual item.\n\n## Using equipment in a form\n\n| Item or effect | Rule |\n|---|---|\n| Original armor and shield | Melded and unusable. Exclude their worn or held AC benefits from the form's normal AC. |\n| Held weapon, tool, focus or material component | Requires actual access and the appropriate anatomy. A form without hands receives no general permission to use it. |\n| Effect requiring an item to be worn, held or equipped | Suspended while the item is melded. |\n| Effect requiring only possession or attunement | May continue only when its exact rule is independent of the lost anatomy and physical use. An unspecified magic bonus is not automatically operative. |\n| Newly acquired armor, harness, collar or container | Must fit the actual size and anatomy and have a legal way to remain attached or carried. Hands are required for held items, not automatically for every wearable item. |\n| Newly acquired external gear when the form changes | Remains external if compatible. Otherwise use the normal drop procedure. It does not silently join an existing meld record. |\n\nThe possession-versus-use distinction and individual compatibility checks are **new provisional defaults**. A specific item or form can supply an explicit exception. Do not infer one from a creature's name, appearance or ability to speak. Item-specific compatibility across the full equipment catalog remains to be authored.\n\n## Returning equipment\n\nResolve a final reversion in this order:\n\n1. Keep current external items that still exist and remain legally equipped or carried by the root body.\n2. Return each surviving melded item that remains in the character's legitimate custody to its recorded former slot or container, if available and legal.\n3. If an external item now occupies that slot, leave it there. Return the former item as carried inventory if there is a legal place and sufficient capacity.\n4. Drop an item that cannot be carried or equipped at the current position or the nearest legal adjacent item position.\n5. If no legal drop position exists, record that item's placement as pending. It continues to exist; it is not duplicated, destroyed or used to displace another creature.\n\nCurrent external equipment taking priority over a former slot is a **new provisional integration rule**. A returned sword may go into available carried storage instead of replacing a sword you actually acquired and equipped during the transformation. Both retain their current condition and ownership.\n\nDestroyed, consumed, physically transferred, lost or otherwise unavailable items never reappear. An item in another custodian's possession cannot return merely because you still claim ownership of it. Someone else's legal ownership does not prevent an intact borrowed item from returning to your continuing legitimate custody.\n\n## Placement and involuntary endings\n\nA transformation does not grant movement. A voluntary size change keeps the same spatial anchor and must fit before payment. Preserve the existing footprint on a same-size change when possible.\n\nA mandatory ending still occurs if the root anatomy does not fit. First try its normal space at the same position, then a legal squeeze under the [movement rules](COMBAT_ACTION_PLAYTEST.md). If neither fits, record a placement conflict. The **new provisional placement procedure** permits only a nearest legal adjacent placement in the same unobstructed region. It cannot pass through a wall, closed barrier or occupied space. Choose the minimum displacement that fits the whole body; among ties, preserve the greatest overlap with its previous footprint. If still tied, the Director uses a fixed neutral board order, not a tactical choice by the affected player. If no adjacent candidate fits, the conflict remains until the obstruction is removed or a legal relocation effect resolves it. Do not search farther afield.\n\nThis mandatory normalization spends no movement and triggers no opportunity attack, charge distance, movement benefit or other movement rider. It changes no remaining movement allowance and grants no invented crushing damage. It does not permit a voluntary form change to bypass its preflight.\n\nTrack actual height and terrain. Losing flight while unsupported starts ordinary falling from the current location; reversion never moves the creature directly to the ground. Ending a burrowing form underground does not move the creature to the surface. Master of Vermin's permission to pass through Tiny gaps changes clearance, not its printed size.\n\nUse the actual space of the expressed anatomy:\n\n| Size | Space | On a grid of 2.5-foot subcells |\n|---|---|---|\n| Tiny | 2.5 by 2.5 feet | 1 subcell |\n| Small | 5 by 5 feet | 2 by 2 subcells |\n| Medium | 5 by 5 feet | 2 by 2 subcells |\n| Large | 10 by 10 feet | 4 by 4 subcells |\n\nV6 explicitly supplies Tiny and Medium space. Small and Large complete this bounded table provisionally; the supplied V6 passage does not separately list them. Size category still matters even when two sizes occupy the same space. Squeezing changes clearance and its penalties; it does not change size, carrying capacity or weight. Exact Tiny occupancy requires subcells or equivalent measurement, not treating Tiny as Medium.\n\n## Body weight and conditions\n\n**New provisional rule:** a creature-form transformation preserves the body's existing base physical weight unless an exact rule changes it. Size alone does not change weight. A 180-pound character therefore remains 180 pounds as a Tiny Hawk or Large Bear. This is a magical transformation rule, not a claim about the weight of ordinary animals. It applies equally to the current 26 Druid and 12 creature-form spell profiles.\n\nRecord base body weight separately from equipment and condition effects. Petrified multiplies current body weight by ten once; it does not rewrite the base weight or multiply repeatedly for multiple applications. Ending or changing a form does not cure that condition. Temporary Druid form ends under its existing Incapacitated rule, then Petrified continues to apply to the reverted body. An anatomy permitted to persist keeps its own base weight and the same condition overlay.\n\nTrue Polymorph object anatomy uses its actual documented output weight. Its output ceiling remains the lower of current effective input weight and earliest recorded origin weight. Include a current applicable weight-changing condition in the input, but never rebase the origin ceiling upward. Equipment, casualty Load and estimated pounds do not establish material available for a transformation. Creature-origin object anatomy has no independent sale, salvage or harvest value.\n\nFor another creature, mount, floor or platform supporting you, count your effective body weight plus all actual equipment in your custody, including melded items, once. For your own inventory capacity, count what you carry, not your own body weight. Conditions change only the items or body their exact rules affect.\n\n## Carrying and Load\n\nUse pounds for actual weight and Load for inventory burden. Extraction Class describes cargo danger and attention. A casualty's Load describes the difficulty of transporting a body; it is not the body's weight. These quantities answer different questions and never convert automatically into each other.\n\nThe following is a **new provisional reconciliation** of V6 physical handling with the staged Gameplay Loop inventory rules. It does not promote the historical Gameplay Loop file into the approved books. V6's optional encumbrance variant remains preserved as an alternative; do not add its penalties to this Load procedure.\n\n| Carrier size | Physical carry limit in pounds | Push, drag or lift limit in pounds | Inventory capacity in Load | Casualty Load for a body of this size |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Tiny | Strength score × 7.5 | Strength score × 15 | Strength score ÷ 2 | 1.5 |\n| Small | Strength score × 15 | Strength score × 30 | Maximum of 0 and Strength score − 2 | 3 |\n| Medium | Strength score × 15 | Strength score × 30 | Strength score | 6 |\n| Large | Strength score × 30 | Strength score × 60 | Strength score × 2 | 12 |\n\nUse the character's current Strength score, not the modifier or an imported monster score. Tiny's Inventory capacity and casualty Load are new provisional additions. The pound limits come from V6; Small, Medium and Large Load values come from the Gameplay Loop. Larger unsupported transport sizes require their own completed rule before this table can handle them.\n\nKeep fractional Loads without rounding. An item with a printed Load uses that value. If it has only a known weight, its **provisional Load is pounds divided by ten**. A Load-only item may use ten pounds per Load as a clearly labeled estimate for ordinary hauling; this is insufficient for effects requiring documented physical mass.\n\nCount a container's own empty Load plus the contents' Loads, unless an explicitly inclusive Load already covers those exact contents. Handle pounds separately: sum the empty container weight and contents' weights, or use an explicitly inclusive gross weight for the contents it covers. An inclusive Load does not make a printed weight inclusive, or vice versa. Apply this rule through nested containers and meld budgets so every item counts exactly once in each quantity.\n\nWith positive carried Load at or above half capacity and no greater than capacity, you are **Burdened**: reduce Speed by 10 feet and apply disadvantage to checks to climb, swim or move silently. An empty inventory is not Burdened, including at zero capacity. Above capacity, that inventory cannot be carried routinely; drop or transfer something, or use a legal alternative such as dragging or a vehicle. These threshold and zero-Load details are provisional clarifications.\n\nApply actual physical limits when handling a known weight. Between physical carry capacity and twice that capacity, pushing or dragging uses the lower of otherwise-current Speed and 5 feet. A Speed of 0 stays 0; a Speed of 3 stays 3. Lifting does not independently permit movement: moving while supporting a weight must satisfy carrying, pushing, dragging or another exact rule. An anatomy restriction, such as Hawk's prohibition on carrying a Medium ally, still applies even if numbers would fit. Burden penalties do not create hands, harnesses, lift or access to melded gear.\n\nTransporting a creature must pass both checks: the carrier can physically handle the body's effective weight and associated equipment, and can accommodate the casualty Load plus the equipment's Load. Petrified multiplies the body weight by ten, not its casualty Load. Removing gear can reduce equipment weight and Load; it cannot reduce the body's weight or erase its condition.\n\n## Meld limits across repeated transformations\n\n**New provisional meld exception:** legally melded gear is excluded from both your transformed body's active Inventory Load and its own active pound-carrying limit. Record its full Load and weight separately while it remains inaccessible. New external equipment uses the expressed form's current capacities and still needs compatible anatomy and custody.\n\nA **continuous meld chain** begins when root anatomy first enters a transformation that melds gear. At that instant, record two fixed ceilings: the pre-transformation body's ordinary active Load capacity and its ordinary physical carrying capacity in pounds. Legally melded gear must fit both. Keep the actual total Load and pounds of the hidden gear, counting containers and contents once in each quantity.\n\nDruid-to-Druid changes, internal Shapechange switches, suppression, permanent True Polymorph and continuous replacement by another spell do not end the chain or increase its ceilings. Later changes to size or Strength neither increase nor retroactively shrink these historical ceilings. An existing item is not expelled merely to make room for a new one.\n\nA later spell replacement may capture an external item only if the whole item fits the remaining room under **both** ceilings. Do not partially meld an item unless its source defines a divisible cargo quantity. A new item that will not fit remains external if compatible with the destination and within its current carrying limits; otherwise drop it or, for a forced resolution without legal placement, record its placement as pending. Excess cargo never grants immunity to a hostile or forced transformation. Druid-to-Druid changes continue to preserve their existing meld and never recapture external cargo.\n\nFor a voluntary change, declare and preflight the full item disposition before spending costs. A successful hostile or forced change resolves its mandatory custody consequences without deleting items or refunding its costs. This same overflow procedure applies at the first forced transformation if the target began with excess cargo; only gear within both starting ceilings can become hidden.\n\nWhen someone else carries the transformed character, count its casualty Load plus both its melded and external equipment Load. Count the effective physical body weight plus all that equipment for actual weight handling. Melding therefore provides no weightless cargo for another carrier.\n\nOnly an actual final return to root anatomy, with the hidden equipment released and reconciled, ends the chain. Returned gear rejoins active inventory under the root body's **current** capacities. Resolve excess with the return-and-drop procedure; overload cannot prevent a mandatory reversion. A later new transformation can then establish a new pair of ceilings from its actual legal starting state.\n\n## Extraction Class and Secure Cargo\n\nExtraction Class is a danger and logistics rating, not a carrying allowance:\n\n| Class | Cargo |\n|---|---|\n| 0 | Ordinary trade goods |\n| 1 | Common monster parts or durable salvage |\n| 2 | Uncommon, fragile or regulated cargo |\n| 3 | Rare artifacts, volatile substances or living captives |\n| 4 | Very rare strategic cargo or dangerous curses |\n| 5 | Epic cargo likely to attract sovereign or faction intervention |\n| 6 | Legendary cargo requiring a dedicated extraction operation |\n\nRetain relevant Fragile, Volatile, Cursed, Living, Contraband, Perishable and Attention tags. A light object can carry a high Extraction Class, and a heavy ordinary crate can remain Class 0.\n\n**New provisional wording for Secure Cargo:** spend an Action on one legal attach, lift, transfer or protective operation involving one discrete cargo unit or bundle with an assigned Extraction Class. The unit must be physically handleable as declared; naming an entire convoy a bundle does not make it one operation. The action grants no extra capacity, hands, equipment, automatic success, invulnerability or reduction in Extraction Class. Apply actual restraints, harnesses, checks, hazards and source-specific handling requirements.\n\n## Examples\n\n**The remote sword.** A Druid wears armor, holds a staff and carries a pack containing two potions. A spare sword is in a wagon. The armor, staff, pack and both potions meld when the Druid changes. The sword remains in the wagon. Neither potion can be consumed from the melded pack.\n\n**A compatible harness.** A transformed creature acquires a harness made for its present anatomy. A later change checks the harness against the destination form. If it does not fit, the voluntary change requires a legal drop. The harness is neither erased nor added to the old melded equipment.\n\n**The crowded hand.** A spell Humanoid acquires and holds a new weapon. When the spell ends, root anatomy can keep that weapon if legal. The original melded weapon returns to an available legal carrying position, or is dropped if necessary. It does not replace or duplicate the new weapon.\n\n**The loaded Hawk.** A Strength 9 Druid becomes Tiny with 3 Load of legally melded gear. It stays inaccessible and uses neither of the Hawk's active carrying capacities. Tiny capacity is 4.5 Load and 67.5 pounds. An additional package of 3 Load and 30 pounds in a genuinely compatible external carrying arrangement fits both capacities, but exceeds half the Load capacity of 2.25 and makes the Druid Burdened. A rescuer carrying that Hawk counts 1.5 casualty Load plus all 6 Load of equipment, totaling 7.5 Load, and separately satisfies the full body and equipment weight.\n\n**The repeated replacement.** A Strength 9 Medium character starts a chain with 8 Load and 80 pounds of legal gear. The fixed ceilings are 9 Load and 135 pounds. The remaining hidden capacity is therefore 1 Load and 55 pounds. A later spell cannot hide a new 3-Load, 30-pound package even if the current form can carry it externally. A whole 0.5-Load, 50-pound item fits both remaining ceilings; a 0.5-Load, 60-pound item fails the pound limit. Changing form or casting again does not refresh either ceiling.\n\n**The stone casualty.** A Medium ally has a documented base weight of 180 pounds and becomes Petrified. The body now weighs 1,800 pounds but still contributes 6 casualty Load. A Strength 18 Medium rescuer can normally carry 270 pounds or push, drag or lift 540 pounds, so cannot move that body alone through these ordinary procedures, even with enough spare Load capacity. Additional gear is counted separately.\n\n## Source decisions and implementation status\n\nThe [first body and equipment conference](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_TEMPORAL_BODY_EQUIPMENT_CONFERENCE.md) supplies the equipment procedures. The [additional exact evidence](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/CODEX_TEMPORAL_CARRYING_EVIDENCE.md) and [GPT's completed reconciliation](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_TEMPORAL_CARRYING_RECONCILIATION.md) preserve the V6, Gameplay Loop and Core conflicts and their dispositions. The corrected candidate above replaces the first conference's full-cell Tiny footprint, factor-four mass proposal and automatic active Load from melded equipment. The manuscript review's chain budgets, pound exemption, borrowed-custody correction, separate inclusive quantities and neutral placement tie rule are applied here. The spell helper's coarse hands check does not implement the full compatibility procedure. The finite ground-plane temporal helper does not model elevation, burrowing or Tiny subcells.
